# Design Inspiration Library

An open reference for web, app, branding and print/social design. **Nothing here is a rule.** Browse it, borrow from it, mix disciplines.

> Generated by `npm run library` from `entries/*.json`. Do not edit by hand — edit the entry JSON/MD and regenerate.

**908 entries**

- [Web (353)](#web)
- [App (172)](#app)
- [Branding (129)](#branding)
- [Print & Social (254)](#print-social)

- **[Pet product site on a laptop resting on a rock with vertical ghost title](entries/web-pet-product-site-on-laptop-with-rock-and-ghost-title.md)** — A green gradient background with a laptop showing a website hero of a dog drinking from a water bottle.
  - Techniques: Prop the device on a natural material that matches the brand story; Use a vertical ghost title to fill space without competing; Match background gradient to the site's hero photo; Keep the mockup front and centre
  - Best for: Outdoor, pet and lifestyle e-commerce; Student and portfolio website presentations; Simple project covers
  - Image: `images/web-pet-product-site-on-laptop-with-rock-and-ghost-title.png`
- **[Real-estate mobile site held in a hand under a giant WEBSITE title on a rust gradient](entries/web-hand-held-phone-real-estate-portfolio-cover-on-rust-gradient.md)** — A rust-brown to red gradient with a huge white 'WEBSITE' at the top edge, a hand holding an angled phone showing a dark interiors site, and small caps captions for category, date and designer at the lower corners.
  - Techniques: Set an oversized white caps word at the top of the frame; Hold the phone at an angle in a hand from below; Use a monochrome warm gradient as the background; Put credits in tiny caps at the corners
  - Best for: Web project covers; Portfolio thumbnails; Property and interiors sites
  - Image: `images/web-hand-held-phone-real-estate-portfolio-cover-on-rust-gradient.png`
- **[Royal blue cover with giant 'WEBSITE' and 'INDUSTRIES' words above and below a phone showing a dark portrait shop](entries/web-blue-project-cover-with-giant-website-industries-and-hero-phone.md)** — A cobalt background with a huge pale-blue 'WEBSITE' at the top and a white heavy 'INDUSTRIES' at the bottom edge, a centred phone showing a dark store hero with a man holding a helmet, dashed circles with sparkle stars…
  - Techniques: Sandwich a phone between two giant words; Trace dashed circles with sparkle nodes; Use one cobalt colour; Add small captions at the sides
  - Best for: E-commerce project covers; Type-sandwich mockups; Blue portfolio thumbnails

- **[White rounded card holding a villa photo and centred title over a beach house background](entries/web-framed-property-card-over-beach-villa-photo.md)** — A full-bleed photo of a palm-lined beach house with a large white rounded rectangle on top.
  - Techniques: Lay a white card over a full-bleed photo to hold text; Repeat the same imagery inside the card at rounded scale; Centre a heavy caps title with a logo tile beneath; Keep body text small and centred
  - Best for: Property development and travel covers; Web project covers with a strong photo; Marketing one-pagers
  - Image: `images/web-framed-property-card-over-beach-villa-photo.png`

### Web › Responsive mobile web (9)

- **[Botanical wellness site on mobile, sage gradient and giant caps](entries/web-botanical-wellness-mobile-typography-screens.md)** — Three phones over a pale sage gradient.

  - Best for: Education and language-learning sites; Youthful community brands; Mobile-first service pages

### Web › E-commerce (7)

- **[Grocery Product Detail with Store Comparison](entries/web-grocery-marketplace-product-detail-page.md)** — A product page in a macOS window frame: deep teal rounded header with search, a large product image on a pale tile with thumbnails, price with superscript cents, Klarna row, lime Buy now button, urgency countdown and a…
  - Techniques: Superscript decimals on prices for a distinct numeric style; Lime pill CTA as the loudest element against teal and white; Countdown and sold-recently badges for urgency; Store comparison rows that put price and delivery time side by side; Rounded pale tile behind the product image

  - Techniques: Dark scrim behind a bright modal for focus; Pie-progress icons to show installments filling up; Stacked collapsible rows to manage density; Black pill for the final confirm to differ from the app's lime CTA; Third-party brand color (pink tag) as a trust marker
  - Best for: Payment and confirmation overlays; Buy-now-pay-later flows; Any high-stakes single decision

- **[Podium Product Display Store Homepage](entries/web-3d-podium-department-store-homepage.md)** — A store homepage on a mustard backdrop with a dark green top bar, a light blue hero where products (bags, luggage, electronics, snacks) sit on stacked yellow podiums, a bold green headline and a row of colorful category…

  - Best for: Multi-category retail sites; Marketplaces; Campaign landing pages with many product types

- **[Product Page with Large Package Tile](entries/web-grocery-product-page-hero-crop.md)** — A full-width product page under a teal header: breadcrumb-style title, a big rounded tile showing a flour bag with a 'Free Delivery' tab, five thumbnails, a countdown, title, rating, superscript price, installment…
  - Techniques: Corner tab badge on the image tile; Thumbnail strip beneath the main image; Price in bold with small raised decimals; Secondary neutral button next to the lime primary; Icon-led reassurance row at the bottom
  - Best for: Product detail pages; Grocery and consumer goods shops; Conversion-focused layouts
  - Image: `images/web-grocery-product-page-hero-crop.png`
- **[Ring Headphones Product Page](entries/web-orange-gradient-monospace-product-page-headphones.md)** — A product page presented on an orange-red gradient: a light gray window with a huge grayscale render of ring-shaped headphones in the center, left panel with description, specs and gallery, right panel with price,…
  - Techniques: Oversized central product render breaking into the side panels; Monospace headings ending in periods for a spec-sheet voice; Single orange accent for CTAs, selected states and progress; Modular side panels split by hairlines; Gradient backdrop framing the window
  - Best for: Hardware and audio product launches; Concept product pages; Design-forward e-commerce
  - Image: `images/web-orange-gradient-monospace-product-page-headphones.png`
- **[Search Suggestion Dropdown over Product Grid](entries/web-grocery-search-dropdown-mega-suggestions.md)** — A grocery site with the search bar active: a wide white dropdown over a dimmed page lists suggestions and stores on the left and product results with images and prices on the right, above a grid of large product cards.
  - Techniques: Two-column dropdown separating text suggestions from rich product results; Bolding the matched part of each suggestion; Dim overlay behind the panel; Tinted rows for product results with thumbnail and price; View all results link as the exit
  - Best for: Search-heavy catalogs; Grocery and marketplace apps; Any site where discovery drives conversion
  - Image: `images/web-grocery-search-dropdown-mega-suggestions.png`
- **[Two-Column Grocery Checkout Page](entries/web-clean-two-column-grocery-checkout.md)** — A checkout screen with a teal rounded header, a left column of cards for delivery information and items grouped by store, and a right column with payment options, promo field, itemized totals, an outlined Klarna button…
  - Techniques: Two-column split with tasks left and summary right; Cards grouped by vendor with collapsible sections; Right-aligned totals with a bold final line; Promo field with an attached dark Apply button; Muted unselected radio options to focus the selected one
  - Best for: Checkout and payment flows; Order summary and review screens; Multi-vendor carts
  - Image: `images/web-clean-two-column-grocery-checkout.png`

### Web › Data visualization (6)

- **[Bento of stat tiles: a black '64% MARKET SHARE' card with diagonal streaks, a '+240% GROWTH' card with rising bars, a '12 AWARDS' tile and a '4.9 / 5.0' star tile](entries/web-black-market-share-tile-with-white-growth-bars-award-and-rating-tiles.md)** — A white page with four rounded tiles: a large black tile with a small pill 'MARKET SHARE', a big white '64%' and a grey line, faint diagonal streaks in its corner, a pale grey wide tile 'GROWTH +240%' with ten rising…

### Print & Social › Product UI promo card (3)

- **[Calendar UI Fragment on Aurora Gradient](entries/print-social-gradient-glow-ui-fragment-promo-calendar.md)** — A wide promo card showing two overlapping white UI panels, a date picker and a time selector, floating on a black field lit by blurred teal, blue and orange color glows at the edges.
  - Techniques: Crop a real interface down to one or two components and enlarge them so the feature reads in a glance; Overlap two cards at different depths to imply a flow (choose a date, then a time); Bleed soft colored glows in from the corners of a near-black field instead of using a full gradient; Use a single saturated accent (the blue selected date) as the only color inside the white UI; Leave generous dark margin so the fragment feels like a spotlit object
  - Best for: Feature announcements and changelog posts on social; Product marketing cards for SaaS tools; Newsletter or blog header images that explain one capability

- **[Fee Breakdown Card on Blue Gradient](entries/print-social-gradient-glow-fee-breakdown-promo.md)** — A promo card with a white panel listing project fees with green savings and a blue net total, plus a smaller overlapping pill card reading 'Plus no platform fee for your clients', over a deep teal-to-blue gradient.
  - Techniques: Use semantic color inside the UI: green for saved, blue for total, black for neutral; Strike-through the fee to show a discount visually; Add a secondary overlapping strip that delivers the punchline; Bottom-weighted gradient that brightens toward the foot of the frame; Large, rounded white cards that keep text legible against the vivid backdrop
  - Best for: Pricing or fee-related announcements; Fintech and marketplace social posts; Explaining a value proposition with concrete numbers
  - Image: `images/print-social-gradient-glow-fee-breakdown-promo.webp`
- **[Portfolio Site and Mobile View on Neon Gradient](entries/print-social-gradient-glow-portfolio-site-promo.md)** — A promo graphic showing a photographer's portfolio website on desktop with a phone view overlapping its edge, a black app-icon tile at the left, on a black background with blue and magenta glows.
  - Techniques: Cropped browser window running off the bottom edge to imply more content; Overlapping a phone frame over the desktop frame to show responsive behavior; Photography as the color payload against neutral UI and a dark surround; Placing a bold brand tile at the left edge to sign the composition; Blue and magenta glows in opposite corners to balance the frame
  - Best for: Announcing a portfolio or website-builder feature; Showcasing customer work in social carousels; Template marketplace promotions
  - Image: `images/print-social-gradient-glow-portfolio-site-promo.webp`

### Print & Social › Social carousel cover (2)

- **[Hot-pink sticker-collage carousel cover](entries/print-social-sticker-collage-carousel-cover.md)** — A 4:5 portrait Instagram carousel cover on flat hot pink.

  - Best for: Instagram and TikTok carousels, especially lifestyle, beauty and fashion content; Listicle and 'rules / tips / steps' posts; Youth-oriented or feminine-leaning brands and creators; Posters, event flyers and zine covers that want a sticker-scrapbook feel
  - Image: `images/print-social-sticker-collage-carousel-cover.png`
- **[Pastel Easter cover with pixel-art stickers and pill labels](entries/print-social-pixel-sticker-easter-carousel-cover.md)** — A 4:5 portrait cover on flat powder blue.
  - Techniques: Mix retro pixel-art icons with a real cut-out photo so digital nostalgia meets human warmth; Use fat rounded display lettering in one saturated blue for a friendly, toy-like voice; Turn text into pill-shaped buttons with thick dark outlines that read as UI chips or stickers; Keep the palette to powder blue, mustard, violet-blue and pink, all soft and matte; Scatter small icons symmetrically around the subject to frame her without crowding
  - Best for: Seasonal social campaigns and holiday carousels; Education, language and lifestyle creators with young audiences; Posters and event flyers with a playful gaming twist
  - Image: `images/print-social-pixel-sticker-easter-carousel-cover.png`

### Print & Social › Animated banner (1)

- **[Cobalt sale banner with ring of gummies and repeating pixel-type discount](entries/print-social-repeating-sale-banner-with-gummy-ring.md)** — A wide cobalt banner.
  - Techniques: Repeat the key offer three times with alternating colours; Combine pixel-style figures with classic serif; Arrange the product in a ring around a label; Animate items rotating for a lively loop
  - Best for: Sales banners and email headers; Motion ads; Supplements and snack brands
  - Image: `images/print-social-repeating-sale-banner-with-gummy-ring.gif`

### Print & Social › Animated story set (1)

- **[Four blue vertical story frames: yellow lunchbox, mint wave transition, glowing brain and a tub with benefit chips](entries/print-social-blue-story-frames-lunchbox-brain-and-wave-transition.md)** — A strip of four tall royal-blue frames on crumpled paper: a serif 'Lunchbox coming back full?' with a yellow 3D lunchbox, a blank frame with a mint wave rising, a glowing pink brain with a light streak and a product tub…
  - Techniques: Use a rising wave as a transition; Show one 3D object per frame; Ring a product with benefit chips; Keep serif headlines with a green keyword
  - Best for: Animated story ads; Storyboard presentations; Child-nutrition promos
  - Image: `images/print-social-blue-story-frames-lunchbox-brain-and-wave-transition.gif`

### Print & Social › Brand social kit (1)

- **[Fintech social profile and post kit with glass-card 3D](entries/print-social-fintech-brand-social-profile-kit.md)** — A presentation board showing a digital-bank brand applied to social media: a Facebook-style profile with a wide banner of translucent gradient glass cards, an orange circular avatar, and two square feed posts with 3D…
  - Techniques: Colour one keyword in the headline with the brand accent so every post has an instant focal point; Use a single abstract 3D render (fanned glass panels, coins) as a wide banner that crops gracefully across formats; Keep a pale grey-white base with soft pastel gradients so the accent colour and avatar pop; Present social assets inside realistic platform chrome so the client sees them in context; Reuse one template (icon + header, 3D object, two-line headline, small subline) across every post
  - Best for: Fintech, banking and payments brands; Brand guideline decks and case studies showing social rollout; Tech brands that need a premium-but-approachable feed
  - Image: `images/print-social-fintech-brand-social-profile-kit.jpg`

### Print & Social › Campaign banner system (1)

- **[Retail launch and seasonal banner set: Target, Black Friday, Cyber Monday, Holiday, New Year](entries/print-social-retail-launch-and-seasonal-banner-system-target.md)** — A long sheet with black tape-style headers dividing five campaign sections, each with three wide banners in royal blue and pale blue: a retailer launch with red basket and bullseye, colour/logo/endcap pages, Black…
  - Techniques: Head each section with a black label strip; Use hang-tag shapes for campaign titles; Repeat round percentage badges; Swap props by season while keeping layout
  - Best for: Retail promotional banner families; Seasonal sale systems; E-commerce marketing kits
  - Image: `images/print-social-retail-launch-and-seasonal-banner-system-target.png`

### Print & Social › Campaign poster (1)

- **[Campaign poster with smiling candidate over sunset landmarks and yellow condensed type](entries/print-social-political-campaign-portrait-with-landmarks.md)** — A warm orange-to-red sunset backdrop with silhouettes of historic buildings and cacti.
  - Techniques: Place a large friendly portrait against a warm sunset gradient; Use tall condensed caps in yellow and white for the slogan; Bring in local landmarks as tinted silhouettes; Add a red name tag with a heart as a brand device; Outline the region's map as a graphic
  - Best for: Political and civic campaigns; Regional identity posters; Personality-led promotions
  - Image: `images/print-social-political-campaign-portrait-with-landmarks.png`

### Print & Social › Campaign social ad set (1)

- **[Black Friday campaign set in cobalt with tags, starbursts and interface parodies](entries/print-social-black-friday-campaign-story-set-cobalt.md)** — A collage of vertical ads for a supplement brand.
  - Techniques: Parody everyday interfaces (calendar, phone call, iMessage) to frame sale messages; Keep one cobalt base and add green, orange and red badge colours; Pile stickers, tags and starbursts on top of mock UI; Use a repeatable strip of campaign phases as a header; Break the pattern with a real product photo with retail props
  - Best for: Seasonal sales and retail campaigns; DTC brands with playful voices; Multi-format story ad sets
  - Image: `images/print-social-black-friday-campaign-story-set-cobalt.png`

### Print & Social › Concept parody post (1)

- **[Weather-app parody with a floating cake and a week of cake slices](entries/print-social-weather-app-parody-cake-forecast-post.md)** — A portrait post that copies a weather app: a cloudy blue sky, 'HOME' and 'Today' at the top, a floating drizzle cake on a stand in place of the temperature, and a weekly forecast row where each day is a cross-section…
  - Techniques: Recreate a familiar app interface using product photography as the data; Replace icons with cake slices in a horizontal week strip; Float the hero product in the sky like a weather symbol; Keep the interface type thin and white for an authentic feel
  - Best for: Food and bakery menus and daily specials; Any brand able to parody a well-known interface; Content series with day-by-day items
  - Image: `images/print-social-weather-app-parody-cake-forecast-post.png`

### Print & Social › Email campaign set (1)

- **[Royal-blue email newsletters for a vitamin brand across back-to-school, Halloween and sale themes](entries/print-social-email-campaign-strips-in-royal-blue-with-seasonal-themes.md)** — Twelve tall email layouts side by side, mostly cobalt and periwinkle.
  - Techniques: Keep a constant blue base and swap seasonal decorations; Use bright orange buttons for every call to action; Present offers as round starburst badges; Combine customer review cards with product hero shots; Section emails with wavy dividers
  - Best for: Ecommerce email design; Seasonal campaign systems; Supplement and family brands

### Print & Social › Event speaker card (1)

- **[Conference speaker card with cut-out portrait and confetti stripes](entries/print-social-conference-speaker-card-indigo-and-lime.md)** — A dark indigo card with a cut-out portrait of a suited man with glasses, a violet 'Speaker' pill, name and title in white, a date block and registration link, coloured pill confetti behind his shoulder, and a row of…
  - Techniques: Cut out the subject and let coloured pill shapes peek behind his shoulders; Use a small role pill above the name as a label; Set date and link in a bordered block at the lower left; Close with a sponsor logo strip
  - Best for: Conference and summit speaker announcements; Panel and webinar promotion; Templated speaker series
  - Image: `images/print-social-conference-speaker-card-indigo-and-lime.jpg`

### Print & Social › Instagram feed grid (1)

- **[Travel agency feed in sky blue with swooshes and circular crops](entries/print-social-travel-agency-feed-grid.md)** — A 4x3 grid of Instagram posts for a travel brand.
  - Techniques: Use one brush-stroke swoosh in the brand blue as a signature device across all posts; Alternate full-bleed photos with circular or arch crops for variety within a system; Keep headline size and placement predictable for scannability; Let a small cloud cutout blend photos with the sky palette
  - Best for: Travel and tourism accounts; Any brand needing a cohesive photo-led feed; Templates for social managers
  - Image: `images/print-social-travel-agency-feed-grid.png`

### Print & Social › Moodboard post (1)

- **[Matcha moodboard with playlist widget, candle and yellow pill](entries/print-social-matcha-moodboard-widget-collage.md)** — A cream 4:5 collage with three rounded blocks: a green playlist widget with a Spotify logo and five matcha photos, a full-width photo of frothy matcha with a floating white candle, and a yellow pill holding cut-outs of…
  - Techniques: Borrow a familiar UI (music widget) as a layout to frame photos; Stack rounded rectangles of different sizes to build a soft modular collage; Keep the palette to green, cream and one sunny yellow; Isolate objects in a pill strip for a product line-up
  - Best for: Lifestyle and wellness moodboards; Candle, ceramic and café brands; Brand story slides
  - Image: `images/print-social-matcha-moodboard-widget-collage.png`

### Print & Social › Pattern (1)

- **[Two-colour crimson and cream fluid swirl pattern with scattered dots](entries/print-social-crimson-and-cream-liquid-swirl-pattern.md)** — A portrait pattern of thick flowing cream ribbons winding through a deep red background, punctuated by cream circles and small dots.
  - Techniques: Use thick organic curves in two colours; Add circles of varying sizes as punctuation; Let shapes bleed off the edges; Use it as a wrap, background or packaging pattern
  - Best for: Packaging and poster backgrounds; Retro or 70s themes; Brand pattern systems
  - Image: `images/print-social-crimson-and-cream-liquid-swirl-pattern.jpg`

### Print & Social › Photographic image (1)

- **[Wooden chess knight pixelated into a mosaic grid, lit by slatted light and shadow on a dark board](entries/print-social-halftone-pixelated-chess-knight-in-slatted-light.md)** — A dark low-key photo of a wooden chess knight on a surface crossed by diagonal light stripes and a long shadow, with a fine square pixel mosaic laid over the whole picture.
  - Techniques: Overlay a fine square mosaic on a photo; Light with slatted stripes; Keep the subject warm against black; Cast a long shadow
  - Best for: Strategy and tech brand imagery; Pixel-effect photography; Dark moody backgrounds
  - Image: `images/print-social-halftone-pixelated-chess-knight-in-slatted-light.webp`

### Print & Social › Poster (1)

- **[Retro poster with huge cream bubble type over a red-toned portrait](entries/print-social-retro-branded-ui-poster-red-and-cream.md)** — A red-framed portrait poster with a man in a red beanie and white round sunglasses.
  - Techniques: Let the subject overlap the giant headline so it appears behind the hat; Tint the whole photo red to match a brand palette; Use a fat rounded 70s-style type in cream; Add small caption and a pill button as a subtle UI layer
  - Best for: Fashion and brand campaigns; Retro-styled posters; UI concept promo posters

### Print & Social › Promo flyer (1)

- **[Course flyer with monochrome portrait, lime bars and a lime circle](entries/print-social-lime-highlighted-portrait-course-flyer.md)** — A white portrait flyer with a greyscale woman pointing at the viewer, a lime bar over her eyes, a large lime circle behind, and a headline 'start Learning today #create design.' where words sit in outlined boxes or lime…
  - Techniques: Use a cutout monochrome portrait with direct gesture as the call to action; Highlight words with lime rectangles and outline boxes; Layer a big lime circle behind the subject; Add a hand-drawn loop around the contact call
  - Best for: Course and workshop flyers; Social ads for training; Design-school promotions

### Print & Social › Recruitment poster (1)

- **[Volunteer call in black with lime pills and giant cropped word](entries/print-social-volunteer-call-lime-and-black-poster.md)** — A white poster with black bold headline 'Call for Volunteers', lime pill labels for roles (Designers, Writers, Video Editors, Content Managers) with megaphone icons, and a huge black word 'Volunteers' cropped at the…
  - Techniques: Show open roles as lime pills; Crop a giant word at the bottom edge for scale and drama; Contrast black type with white and lime; Use small pointing icons to guide reading
  - Best for: Volunteer and hiring drives; Community and student organisations; Announcement posters

### Print & Social › Retail display graphics (1)

- **[Back-to-school shelf shippers, end cap and flag display concepts](entries/print-social-retail-shelf-shipper-display-concepts.md)** — A presentation of in-store display concepts for a gummy vitamin brand.
  - Techniques: Use a seasonal icon (school bus, crayons) as a topper to turn shelving into a character; Colour-code shelf bands by product to organise a wall; Label sections with black tape-like strips against a crumpled paper backdrop; Make a tall display echo a familiar shape (backpack) with zippers and pockets
  - Best for: Retail merchandising and point-of-purchase; Seasonal campaigns; Packaging and display concept decks

### Print & Social › Social infographic (1)

- **[Four-step cake ordering guide on a wavy white path with pink starburst numbers](entries/print-social-cobalt-cake-order-steps-on-wavy-path.md)** — A cobalt 4:5 post where a winding white line links four cake-slice cut-outs, each with a pink serrated 'Step N' badge and a bold title with a one-sentence description in white.
  - Techniques: Use one continuous curved line to link steps in a zigzag; Show a different product image at each step; Number steps with pink starburst badges; Add a handwritten reassurance note
  - Best for: How-to-order and process explainers; Food and service businesses; Step-by-step carousels

- **[Violet quote card 'Time waits for no one' with a violet 3D alarm clock rising from the bottom](entries/print-social-purple-time-waits-quote-post-with-alarm-clock-render.md)** — A violet square with a small URL and logo, a white rounded card with three dots at the top and a quote 'Time waits for no one, but you have the power to make the most of it.' with a short underline, and a glossy violet…
  - Techniques: Put the quote on a white window-like card; Use a monochrome 3D object matching the background; Crop the object at the bottom; Underline a short accent line
  - Best for: Quote and motivation posts; Monochrome 3D social graphics; Violet brands
  - Image: `images/print-social-purple-time-waits-quote-post-with-alarm-clock-render.jpg`

### Print & Social › Story mockups (1)

- **[Three Instagram stories on black: a man hailing the van, 'AI drives. You relax.' and parked vans in a city](entries/print-social-black-story-mockups-of-autonomous-vans-with-light-captions.md)** — A black board with an outlined 'STORIES EXAMPLE' pill and three story frames with a lime avatar 'Pilotaxi', progress bars and message bars: a man checking a lime app beside a white van, a van driving toward the camera…
  - Techniques: Use full-bleed city photography; Put a single thin headline in one story; Keep story chrome authentic; Use a lime avatar as the only colour

### Print & Social › Story set (1)

- **[Three soft pastel story cards for a fintech app](entries/print-social-fintech-story-cards-pastel-blue.md)** — Three tall rounded story cards with progress bars at the top and brand header.
  - Techniques: Use a soft sky-blue gradient across all cards; Pair one photographic card with two 3D or glass object cards; Add story progress bars to sell the format; Keep headlines short two-liners with contrasting statements
  - Best for: Fintech app promotion; Instagram or in-app stories; Cohesive three-part sequences

### Print & Social › Typographic post (1)

- **[Six rows of pink italic serif POPCORN on blue behind a striped popcorn bucket](entries/print-social-repeated-italic-serif-word-behind-cutout-popcorn-bucket.md)** — A dusty blue portrait with the word POPCORN repeated six times in large pink italic serif capitals, filling the frame.
  - Techniques: Repeat one word to fill the frame as a pattern; Pair pink and blue pastels; Let the product cut-out overlap the text rows; Use elegant italic serif for a soft, cinematic tone
  - Best for: Food and snack promotions; Minimal typographic posts; Product-plus-word posters
